# 控件对齐缺口全量清单

由 16 个枚举 agent 逐控件把本库的 published/public 成员面与 LCL/Delphi 同名控件并排比对生成。

## 这份清单和上一份的关系

上一轮审计报了 **44 条**,因为它的第二类("能力根本不存在")被显式截断成 `highest-value only`,
只留了 3 条全局项 —— 而"一个控件十几条"的缺口全都属于那一类。这一轮把那一类完整枚举了。

| | 上一轮 | 这一轮 |
|---|---|---|
| 语义/默认值/类型/改名不一致 | 28 | 225 |
| 能力根本不存在(属性/事件/方法/子对象) | 3(截断) | 472 |
| **合计** | **44** | **697** |

## 可信度 —— 先读这一节

对抗性验证只跑完 **78 / 697 条(11%)** 就撞上会话额度上限。已验证的那批里:

- 真:**50**
- 假:**28** —— 误报率 **36%**

所以 **697 这个数字要打折看**。按已测得的误报率外推,能站住的大约是 **447 条上下**,
但这只是外推,不是结论 —— 剩下 619 条**每一条都还没被质疑过**。
误报主要来自三类:本库其实已有(在基类或别的单元里)、LCL 那侧引用的行号/类不对、
以及对自绘控件无意义的成员(ParentColor / DoubleBuffered 之类)。

## 按成本

- **SMALL**: 387
- **MEDIUM**: 261
- **LARGE**: 49

---

## 逐控件明细

### `TTyStringGrid`  (对标 `TCustomGrid`) — 44 条

| 成员 | 类型 | 那边做什么 | 用户因此做不到什么 | 成本 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `OnPickListSelect` | 事件 | Fires when the user picks an item from a cell's drop-down pick list. | Cascading drop-downs ('choose a country, now reload the city list') have no hook to trigger on. | SMALL |
| `OnGetEditMask` | 事件 | Supplies a per-CELL edit mask on demand. | A settings-style grid where the mask depends on the row (phone here, postcode there) needs one column per format. | SMALL |
| `OnUserCheckboxBitmap / OnUserCheckboxImage` | 事件 | Let the host supply the bitmap / image-list entry used to draw a checkbox cell in a given state. | Tri-state or domain-specific marks (a lock icon, a partial tick) cannot replace the standard check glyph per cell. | SMALL |
| `AutoAdjustColumns / AutoSizeColumns` | 方法 | Auto-fit EVERY column to its content in one call (LCL has both the TCustomGrid virtual and the string-grid pair AutoSizeColumn / AutoSizeColumns). | 'fit all columns to the data I just loaded' - the normal thing to do after LoadFromCSV - needs a host loop, and the obvious AutoSizeColumns name is missing while AutoFitRows exists. | SMALL |
| `SortColRow(IsColumn=False, ...)` | 方法 | Sort by a ROW - i.e. reorder the COLUMNS according to the values in one row - and the range-limited overload SortColRow(IsColumn, Index, FromIndex, ToIndex). | Transposed / matrix-style grids cannot be sorted along the other axis, and no sort can be limited to a sub-range of rows. | SMALL |
| `InsertRowWithValues(Index; Values: array of String)` | 方法 | Insert a row and fill it from an open array in one call - the standard way to populate a string grid. | Table-building code triples in length, and each row insert costs a separate undo/repaint cycle unless the host remembers BeginUpdate. | SMALL |
| `AutoEdit` | 属性 | When True a plain click on the focused cell opens the editor straight away; when False editing needs F2 / a second click. | A grid used mainly for browsing cannot suppress accidental edit-on-click, and a fast-entry grid cannot turn it on. | SMALL |
| `RangeSelectMode: TRangeSelectMode` | 属性 | Chooses rsmSingle (one selection rectangle) or rsmMulti (Ctrl-click builds several disjoint rectangles). | An app whose logic assumes exactly one selected block (the LCL default) gets multi-range behaviour it cannot switch off, and its 'the selection' code silently sees only the last block. | SMALL |
| `SelectActive` | 属性 | Read/write flag saying a rubber-band selection is in progress; setting it starts/stops range extension from code (what Shift+arrow does internally). | Code that drives selection extension programmatically (e.g. a 'select to end' command) cannot put the grid into extend mode. | SMALL |
| `ExtendedSelect` | 属性 | Whether Shift+click / Shift+arrow extends the selection at all. | A single-cell-only grid cannot forbid range selection. | SMALL |
| `FastEditing` | 属性 | Typing a printable character on a focused cell immediately opens the editor and seeds it with that character (spreadsheet type-over). | Data-entry users cannot get (or turn off) the Excel-like 'just start typing' behaviour. | SMALL |
| `StrictSort` | 属性 | Forces a strict (total-order, no equal-keys shortcut) comparison during sorting. | Rows with equal keys can reorder unpredictably between sorts with no way to demand determinism. | SMALL |
| `Modified: Boolean` | 属性 | Read/write dirty flag set by any cell edit; the grid maintains it so the app can prompt 'save changes?'. | Every host reimplements the dirty flag by hooking OnCellEdited, and any edit path we add later (paste, fill handle, undo) has to be remembered again in each app. | SMALL |
| `SaveToCSVStream(..., VisibleColumnsOnly) / LoadFromCSVStream(..., SkipEmptyLines)` | 属性 | Skip hidden columns when exporting; skip blank lines when importing. | Exporting a grid whose columns the user hid still dumps the hidden data - a surprise and, for some data, a leak; and a CSV with blank separator lines imports phantom empty rows. | SMALL |
| `Selection: TGridRect (writable)` | 改名 | Read AND write the selection rectangle in one property; assigning it moves the selection (and, with goSelectionActive, the cursor too). | 'Grid.Selection := R' - a normal LCL idiom, including round-tripping a saved rect - has to be rewritten as SelectRange with four arguments. | SMALL |
| `ClearSelections` | 改名 | Drops all committed selection ranges (plural - the multi-select list). | One-character porting break on a routinely-called method. | SMALL |
| `EditorMode: Boolean (writable)` | 改名 | Read/write property: setting True opens the in-place editor on the current cell, False closes it; reading tells you whether it is open. | 'Grid.EditorMode := True' is the standard way to drop into edit mode from a toolbar button; it needs rewriting, and there is no single settable property to bind to. | SMALL |
| `Clear` | 改名 | Empties the whole grid: removes non-fixed rows AND columns and their content in one call. | Grid.Clear - probably the single most-typed grid call - does not exist, and the nearest name does something narrower, so a port that maps Clear->ClearCells leaves the old columns behind. | SMALL |
| `DeleteCol / DeleteColRow / InsertColRow / ExchangeColRow / MoveColRow` | 改名 | The IsColumn-parameterised structural family, so one call site handles both axes, plus the singular DeleteCol name. | Generic 'delete whichever axis the user picked' code must be un-generalised; DeleteCol->DeleteColumn is a silent compile break; and swapping two COLUMNS has no method at all. | SMALL |
| `HideSortArrow` | 改名 | Clears the sort indicator from the header without changing the row order. | Server-side-sorted grids (where the host sorted the data and only wants the glyph managed) cannot show or hide the arrow independently of our own sorting. | SMALL |
| `Clean / Clean(CleanOptions: TGridZoneSet) / Clean(aRect, ...) / Clean(StartCol,StartRow,EndCol,EndRow, ...)` | 改名 | Four overloads that blank cell content, optionally restricted to a rectangle AND to grid zones (gzNormal / gzFixedCols / gzFixedRows / gzFixedCells) - so 'clear the data but keep the header captions' is one call. | Clearing a rectangular block needs nested loops, and 'clear data rows but not the fixed header row' cannot be expressed at all. | SMALL |
| `CopyToClipboard(AUseSelection: boolean = false)` | 改名 | Copy the WHOLE grid, or just the selection, depending on the argument. | An 'Export all to clipboard' command has to disturb and then restore the user's selection. | SMALL |
| `OnButtonClick / OnEditButtonClick` | 改名 | Fires when the button in a cbsButton / cbsEllipsis / cbsButtonColumn cell is pressed, with (aCol, aRow). | Two names to discover instead of one, and neither matches ported code. | SMALL |
| `OnCheckboxToggled` | 改名 | Fires after a checkbox cell is toggled, with the new state. | Straight rename break on a commonly used event. | SMALL |
| `OnCellProcess: TCellProcessEvent` | 改名 | One event that transforms a cell's text on BOTH copy and paste (cpCopy / cpPaste discriminated by a parameter). | 'strip the currency symbol on copy' has to be done by rewriting the whole clipboard blob instead of per cell. | SMALL |
| `SortOrder: TSortOrder (writable)` | 类型 | Read/WRITE the current sort direction; assigning it flips the sort without naming a column. | Different name and read-only: 'Grid.SortOrder := soDescending' cannot be ported, and there is no way to reverse the existing sort without re-specifying the column. | SMALL |
| `OnBeforeSelection / OnAfterSelection / OnSelection` | 类型 | Three TOnSelectEvent hooks (Sender; aCol, aRow) around a selection change: before it commits, after it commits, and during rubber-banding. | A handler must read Col/Row back off the grid instead of receiving them, cannot tell what the selection WAS, and gets no during-drag feedback - so live 'sum of selection' status bars update only on mouse-up. | SMALL |
| `OnColRowDeleted / OnColRowInserted / OnColRowExchanged / OnColRowMoved` | 事件 | Four AFTER-the-fact structural notifications (IsColumn, index / FromIndex, ToIndex) so the host can keep its parallel data in step. | A host keeping its own array beside the grid gets told it MAY delete a row but never that the deletion happened, so it must guess or re-scan; and code ported from the LCL events silently stops running. | MEDIUM |
| `OnGetEditText / OnSetEditText` | 事件 | The editor's value is fetched from and written back to the host, letting the string shown in the editor differ from the cell's display text, and letting the grid edit data it does not store. | A virtual grid backed by the host's own data cannot be made editable through TTyDrawGrid at all, and 'edit the raw number while displaying a formatted one' has no hook. | MEDIUM |
| `OnGetCheckboxState / OnSetCheckboxState` | 事件 | The host supplies and receives the checkbox state of a cell (TCheckboxState) instead of the grid deriving it from cell text. | Checkbox columns must be backed by our string convention; a host holding real booleans cannot feed them in, and a virtual grid cannot have working checkboxes at all. | MEDIUM |
| `SelectedRange[AIndex] / SelectedRangeCount / HasMultiSelection` | 方法 | Enumerate the disjoint selection rectangles: how many there are, each one's TGridRect, and a quick 'is more than one thing selected'. | We let the user Ctrl-click several blocks and then give the host no way to find out what they picked - a host iterating 'the selection' processes only the last rectangle and silently drops the rest. | MEDIUM |
| `SaveToFile / LoadFromFile + SaveOptions: TSaveOptions + OnSaveColumn / OnLoadColumn` | 方法 | Persist the whole grid to an XML file and read it back, choosing what to include (soDesign = col/row count and Options, soAttributes = font/brush/text style, soContent = cell text, soPosition = cursor and selection), with per-column hooks so an app can add its | 'Grid.SaveToFile(f) / LoadFromFile(f)' - the one-line way to persist a whole grid including its content and cursor - has no counterpart; the host must roll its own format and cannot round-trip attributes or cursor position at all. | MEDIUM |
| `DefaultDrawCell` | 方法 | Lets an OnDrawCell handler call the grid's own default painting for that cell, then draw on top of it. | The common 'draw the normal cell, then stamp an icon in the corner' pattern forces the host to re-implement text drawing, alignment and ellipsis from scratch. | MEDIUM |
| `AutoAdvance: TAutoAdvance / TabAdvance: TAutoAdvance` | 属性 | Where the cell cursor goes after a commit (aaNone, aaDown, aaRight, aaLeft, aaRightDown, aaLeftDown, aaRightUp, aaLeftUp), with a separate direction for the Tab key. | Data-entry grids that must advance down a column after Enter (the classic ledger pattern) cannot be configured - the host has to intercept keys itself. | MEDIUM |
| `CellHintPriority + goTruncCellHints + GetTruncCellHintText` | 属性 | Automatically hints the FULL text of a cell whose text is truncated, and chooses how that combines with the control Hint and OnGetCellHint (chpAll / chpAllNoDefault / chpTruncOnly). | Narrow columns clip their content to '...' and the user has no way to read it - the single most-noticed missing affordance in a grid with fixed column widths. | MEDIUM |
| `Objects[ACol, ARow]: TObject` | 属性 | An arbitrary object pointer stored per cell alongside its text - the standard place to hang the record a row came from. | 'which database record is this row?' has no storage; hosts must keep a parallel array keyed by row index and re-sync it on every insert, delete, sort and move - exactly the bookkeeping the grid already does for its own cells. | LARGE |
| `Cols[index]: TStrings / Rows[index]: TStrings (TStringGridStrings)` | 子对象 | A whole column or row exposed as a live, ASSIGNABLE TStrings - 'Grid.Rows[3] := MyList', 'Memo.Lines := Grid.Cols[0]', Rows[r].CommaText, and per-cell Objects through it. The item class TStringGridStrings does not exist on our side at all. | The whole idiom of filling or reading a grid row-at-a-time (and of piping a row into any TStrings consumer) is unavailable; every port has to be rewritten as a per-cell loop. | LARGE |
| `OnSelectEditor` | 改名 | Fires with (Sender; aCol, aRow; var Editor: TWinControl) so the host can swap in a different editor control for one cell by simple assignment. | Swapping an editor per cell goes from a one-line var-assignment to writing and owning a class; a very common LCL pattern does not port mechanically. | MEDIUM |

| `OnPaintBackground` | 事件 | Called during Paint with the canvas and the computed destination rect, BEFORE the image is drawn, so the host can paint a checkerboard/matte/gradient behind a transparent PNG. | The standard 'alpha checkerboard behind a transparent image' pattern is impossible on this control; the user must drop a TTyPaintPanel behind it and keep the two rects in sync by hand. | SMALL |

| `OnMouseWheelHorz / OnMouseWheelLeft / OnMouseWheelRight` | 事件 | Horizontal wheel / trackpad side-scroll notifications, separate from the vertical trio. | Trackpad and tilt-wheel horizontal gestures over an image are unreachable — notable for a control people pan; the whole library is missing the horizontal half of wheel handling. | SMALL |
| `DestRect` | 方法 | Public virtual function returning the rect (in client coords) the picture actually occupies right now — the basis for hit-testing a click back to an image pixel, drawing selection handles, or overriding placement. | 'Which image pixel did the user click?' needs the user to re-implement the fit maths, and a descendant cannot change placement by overriding one function. | SMALL |
| `ImageIndex` | 属性 | Selects which entry of Images is shown (default 0); changing it repaints. | No way to say 'show icon 3 of the shared list'; a ported .lfm carrying ImageIndex= fails to stream. | SMALL |
| `ImageWidth` | 属性 | Picks WHICH resolution of the image list to draw (a specific logical width, 0 = the list's default), so the same control can request 16px or 32px art. | Even once Images exists there is no way to ask for a specific icon size; the control cannot express 'draw the 24px variant here'. | SMALL |
| `AntialiasingMode` | 属性 | Chooses the scaling/AA quality used when the picture is drawn scaled (amDontCare/amOn/amOff), i.e. smooth interpolation vs hard nearest-neighbour edges. | Pixel art / QR codes / sprite sheets scaled up come out blurred with no way to ask for crisp nearest-neighbour, and a ported .lfm with AntialiasingMode= will not stream. | SMALL |
| `StretchInEnabled` | 属性 | Default True. Gate on ENLARGING: when False a picture smaller than the control is never scaled up, even with Stretch/Proportional set. | Small icons in a large placeholder get upscaled to mush with no opt-out; the standard LCL recipe 'shrink big photos, never enlarge small ones' is unexpressible. | SMALL |
| `StretchOutEnabled` | 属性 | Default True. Gate on SHRINKING: when False a picture larger than the control is not scaled down (it clips instead). | Cannot ask for 'show at 1:1 and clip if too big' while still letting Stretch/Proportional handle the other direction; ported .lfm values silently vanish. | SMALL |
| `KeepOriginXWhenClipped / KeepOriginYWhenClipped` | 属性 | Two published Booleans (default False). When Center is on and the picture is BIGGER than the control, centring would push the origin negative and cut off the top/left; these pin the origin at 0 on that axis so the top-left of the image stays visible. | Showing the top-left corner of an oversized image (maps, screenshots, scans) is impossible: it is always centre-cropped. | SMALL |
| `HasGraphic` | 属性 | Public read-only Boolean: True when there is anything to draw (a picture OR a valid image-list entry). Guards Paint and AutoSize. | Host code must reach into Image.Picture.Graphic and null/Empty-check it by hand to decide whether to show a placeholder. | SMALL |
| `Center` | 默认值 | Whether an unscaled picture is centred in the client area or placed at the top-left. | Silent layout change when porting: a .lfm converted from TImage does not store Center (it was the default False), so on TTyImage the image jumps to the middle of the control instead of the top-left, and pixel-exact layouts shift. | SMALL |
| `Proportional` | 语义 | LCL's Proportional only engages when the picture is bigger than the control (PicOutsidePartial) unless Stretch is also set — a small picture stays at native size. It is a 'shrink to fit, never grow' switch. | Identical .lfm, different picture: a 32x32 logo in a 200x200 Proportional image stays 32x32 under LCL but is upscaled to 200x200 here — blurry logos appear after a port with nothing in the form file to blame. | SMALL |
| `Paint (design-time placeholder frame)` | 语义 | At design time TCustomImage always outlines itself with a black dashed rectangle so an empty image control is visible and selectable on the form. | A freshly dropped or not-yet-assigned TTyImage is completely invisible in the Lazarus form designer — it cannot be found, clicked or resized without going through the Object Inspector's component list. | SMALL |
| `OnDragDrop / OnDragOver / OnEndDrag / OnStartDrag` | 事件 | The four LCL drag-and-drop events that make a control a drop target and a drag source. | 'Drop a picture file / another image here' cannot be built on TTyImage from the designer, although TTyMemo in the same library already publishes exactly these. | MEDIUM |
| `Images` | 属性 | Lets the control display one entry from an image list instead of a TPicture; the list is the DPI-aware icon source, and AutoSize/DestRect fall back to the list's size when Picture is empty. | A single themed icon on a form cannot be sourced from the app's shared image list; the user must duplicate the bitmap into the control's Picture, losing the one-place-to-change icon set and the per-DPI rendering the collection already does. | MEDIUM |
| `DragMode / DragCursor` | 属性 | Published LCL drag-and-drop configuration: dmAutomatic starts a drag on mouse-down, DragCursor sets the cursor shown while dragging. | An image cannot be made a drag source from the Object Inspector; drag-to-reorder / drag-an-image-onto-a-target UIs need hand-written mouse code. | MEDIUM |
| `Transparent` | 语义 | On LCL, Transparent is pushed into the GRAPHIC (Picture.Graphic.Transparent), i.e. it turns the bitmap's own mask/transparent colour on so the form shows through the image's background pixels; default False. | Same property name, opposite default and different subject: setting Transparent=False on a masked BMP under LCL makes the mask colour paint solid; here it paints the panel background instead and the bitmap's mask is never touched, so masked/keyed bitmaps rende | MEDIUM |
| `Canvas` | 语义 | On TCustomImage, Canvas is REDIRECTED to the picture's own bitmap canvas (auto-creating a TBitmap the size of the control if Picture is empty), so 'Image1.Canvas.LineTo(...)' draws INTO the image and persists. | The very common LCL idiom 'use a TImage as a scratch drawing surface' compiles here and then silently loses every stroke at the next repaint, because the same property name now means the transient control canvas. | MEDIUM |

| `PageIndex: Integer (read/write)` | 属性 | A page's position in the pager, ASSIGNABLE — setting it moves the page (and its tab) to that position. | Pages can only be reordered by the user dragging tabs; code cannot move a page (or insert one at a position) at all, so ordering that depends on data — most recently used tabs, sorted documents — is impossible. | MEDIUM |

## 覆盖漏洞(各枚举 agent 自报)

沉默跳过的控件读起来像干净的控件,所以这些必须显式列出:

- All sources opened and read in full: ours — tyControls.Base.pas (1209 lines, so the inherited published surface every ty control gets for free is accounted for: Base.pas:101-141 graphic, 196-246 windowed), tyControls.Edit.pas (1760), tyControls.MaskEdit.pas (234), and tyControls.Memo.pas interface + constructor (lines 1-679; the declaration of every public/published/protected member is inside that range, published block 513-587, public 474-512). Theirs — stdctrls.pp:740-1099 (TCustomEdit 764-880, TMemoScrollbar 885-897, TCustomMemo 902-946, TEdit 951-1015, TMemo 1020-1082), customedit.inc (all 743 lines), custommemo.inc (all 285), maskedit.pp:1-420 (TCustomMaskEdit 206-322, TMaskEdit 326-391); maskedit.pp's 2000+ implementation lines were consulted only through the declarations and the design notes at 18-49 and 167-199, so no claim here rests on its method bodies. Every "absent" call was checked with a case-insensitive grep across ALL of D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/ (not just the three units) plus synonyms — EchoMode, AutoSelect, Modified/IsModified/Dirty, SelectAllOnFocus, AddHandlerOnChange, Append/AddLine, VertScrollBar/HorzScrollBar, MouseWheelHorz, BidiMode, EditText, IsMasked, SpaceChar, ValidateEdit, EnableSets, ValidationError, Accessible — and the grep result is quoted in each ours_evidence. DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED as out of scope per the brief: Color, BorderStyle, ParentColor, ParentFont, DoubleBuffered, ParentDoubleBuffered (self-drawn/themed — StyleClass + theme tokens are the equivalent), EmulatedTextHintStatus / CanShowEmulatedTextHint / ShowEmulatedTextHint (widgetset text-hint emulation internals; we always draw our own hint), OnEndDock/OnStartDock on TMaskEdit (docking plumbing), and all protected-only LCL members (Change, Reset, CheckCursor, GetSel/SetSel, EditCanModify) except where their absence changes user-visible behaviour. ONE small finding had to be dropped to fit the 32-entry cap, recorded here instead: TCustomEdit.EditingDone is suppressed while ReadOnly (customedit.inc:647-651 'if not ReadOnly then inherited EditingDone'), whereas TTyEdit fires EditingDone on Enter unconditionally (tyControls.Edit.pas:1377-1381) — a read-only field therefore runs the form's commit handler.
- All ten files opened successfully and read in full: our four units (D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/tyControls.CheckBox.pas, tyControls.CheckGroup.pas, tyControls.RadioGroup.pas, tyControls.GroupBox.pas) plus D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/tyControls.Base.pas for the inherited base surface, and on their side C:/lazarus/lcl/stdctrls.pp (TButtonControl 1177-1196, TCustomGroupBox/TGroupBox 165-244, TCustomCheckBox 1326-1364, TCheckBox 1370-1428, TToggleBox 1432-1486, TRadioButton 1491-1551), C:/lazarus/lcl/extctrls.pp (TCustomRadioGroup/TRadioGroup 717-847, TCustomCheckGroup/TCheckGroup 854-970), and all four .inc files (customcheckbox.inc, customcheckgroup.inc, radiogroup.inc, customgroupbox.inc). C:/lazarus/lcl/controls.pp was consulted for the visibility of ClientWidth/ClientHeight (public, 1796-1799), BiDiMode (public, 1834), DragKind/DragMode (1571-1572) and DockSite (2336).  Every "absent" claim was checked with a case-insensitive grep across the WHOLE of D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/, not just the four units. Greps that returned nothing anywhere in source/: TLeftRight, BidiMode-as-property, IsRightToLeft/RightToLeft/bdRightToLeft, ChildSizing, ClientWidth/ClientHeight, AutoFill, CheckEnabled, CanModify, OnItemEnter, OnItemExit, GetActionLinkClass/ActionLink/IsCheckedLinked, DefineProperties/ReadData/WriteData, DockSite/OnDockDrop/OnGetSiteInfo. Greps that found the capability wired elsewhere (and are reported as not-wired rather than unimplemented): the drag surface exists on tyControls.Memo.pas:566-574; OnItemClick as an LCL-named event exists on tyControls.ListGroupPanel.pas:216; a latching toggle exists as tyControls.Button.pas:159 (Down) and tyControls.ToggleSwitch.pas:74/87 (Toggle/Checked); Toggle-as-a-method exists on six other controls but not on TTyCheckBox; ColumnLayout appears only inside a comment at tyControls.RadioGroup.pas:21.  Two deliberate scope calls. (2) TCustomCheckBox.Toggle is NOT reported: it is protected in LCL (stdctrls.pp:1344), and setting Checked/State is the obvious equivalent on our side (tyControls.CheckBox.pas:59-61). Everything else is one entry per member per pairing. Nothing was left unexamined; no comparison here is inferred without a file:line on both sides.

Scoping decisions worth knowing: (1) The schema caps entries at 32, so tightly-related members are merged into single entries and the `member` field lists every merged name — the tab-icon feature (Images/ImagesWidth/ImageIndex/OnGetImageIndex/GetImageIndex), the multi-row band (MultiLine/RaggedRight/ScrollOpposite/RowCount), hit-testing (IndexOfTabAt/IndexOfPageAt/GetHitTestInfoAt), the visible-vs-page index mapping (TabToPageIndex/PageToTabIndex/VisibleIndex/TTabSheet.TabIndex), the misc public API (IndexOf/CanChangePageIndex/GetMinimumTabWidth/GetMinimumTabHeight/DoCloseTabClicked/IndexOfTabWithCaption), the behaviour flags (HotTrack/MultiSelect/OwnerDraw), the geometry accessors (DisplayRect/TabRect), TWinControl layout (AutoSize/BorderWidth/ChildSizing), drag-and-drop, docking, and the page's stored-False bounds group. Nothing was dropped for being low value. (2) NOT reported, because a real counterpart exists: TCustomTabControl.Tabs: TStrings and TabIndex — TTyTabSet is this library's TTabControl analogue and publishes both (tyControls.TabSet.pas:35-36); TPageControl.Pages[Index]: TTabSheet (comctrls.pp:609) matches TTyPageControl.Pages[AIndex] exactly (tyControls.PageControl.pas:40); PageCount (theirs a property at :501, ours a function at :39 — identical at the call site); Page[Index] (a synonym of Pages[] on the concrete pager); CustomPage(Index) (same); GetCapabilities (pure widgetset capability query); Canvas (platform-gated, and TCustomControl gives us one anyway); ParentFont/ParentBackground-style members (theme-locked by design); TCustomPage.Flags/CanTab/IsControlVisible/HandleObjectShouldBeVisible/CheckNewParent/WSRegisterClass and all Do*/CN*/CM* handlers (handle plumbing). (3) Engine-level gaps are attributed to TTyCustomTabStrip where the missing member belongs on the shared header engine, meaning both TTyPageControl and TTyTabSet lack it; TTyTabStrip's own additions (OnReorder, TabsClosable, TyTabHoverClose, AnimationsEnabled, header scroll API) have no LCL counterpart and are outside this diff's direction.

TWO CAP-DRIVEN CONSOLIDATIONS to note, because they hide real member counts: the 32-entry limit forced tightly-related members into single entries (e.g. the coolbar band-background entry covers 8 members across TCoolBand and TCustomCoolBar; the statusbar API entry covers 10). Every member so folded is named explicitly in that entry's `member` field, so nothing is silently dropped — but a per-member count would be roughly 110 gaps, not 32. Two of the paths given in the task do not exist and I used the real LCL filenames instead: C:/lazarus/lcl/include/customtrackbar.inc -> trackbar.inc, and C:/lazarus/lcl/include/customscrollbar.inc -> scrollbar.inc (verified by listing C:/lazarus/lcl/include). progressbar.inc, spinedit.inc and customupdown.inc existed as given. On our side I read tyControls.Base.pas first (both base classes' published surface) plus the five target units, and grepped the whole of D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/ case-insensitively for every candidate name before calling anything absent; several claims changed as a result (Style/pbstMarquee became a name-mismatch because TTyActivityBar is the indeterminate sibling; BarShowText, TextHint, CaretPos, SelStart/SelText, clipboard and undo were downgraded from "missing capability" to "built elsewhere, not wired here" citing TTyGauge/TTyCircularProgress/TTyTrackBar, TTyEdit and tyControls.UndoStack.pas; TTyNumericEdit was checked before reporting the float spin gap; the earlier -i hits for "Reversed" and "TickMarks" turned out to be a prose comment in Chart.pas and a local variable tickMarkS in Meter.pas, not properties).  Deliberately not reported, per the task's exclusions: widgetset/handle plumbing (WSRegisterClass, InitializeWnd, CreateParams/CreateWnd, message handlers, ApplyChanges, FixParams, Changed/Scroll/Click protected virtuals, GetControlClassDefaultSize, CalculatePreferredSize); Color / ParentColor / ParentFont / ParentBackground / DoubleBuffered / ParentDoubleBuffered / Ctl3D; BorderWidth (TProgressBar) and BorderStyle (TSpinEdit) because the themed equivalents already exist as border-width / border tokens resolved through CurrentStyle; and on the spin edit CharCase, EchoMode, PasswordChar, HideSelection and NumbersOnly as meaningless for a digits-only numeric field (note NumbersOnly and CharCase do exist on TTyEdit if they are ever wanted). Also not reported, as borderline infrastructure rather than user surface: TCustomEdit.AddHandlerOnChange / RemoveHandlerOnChange (multi-subscriber change list, stdctrls.pp:853-855), TScrollBar's SmallChange/LargeChange range type TScrollBarInc (1..32767) vs our plain Integer, TCustomScrollBar.PageSize's LCL default of 0 vs our 10, TCustomTrackBar.Position having no declared default vs our 0, and LCL's spin-edit key filter accepting a leading '+' and 'e'/'E' (spinedit.inc:136) where ours accepts only digits and one leading '-' (SpinEdit.pas:335-336).  One structural note that spans several entries rather than being one gap: TTyProgressBar and TTyUpDown are TTyGraphicControl descendants while TCustomProgressBar is a TWinControl and TCustomUpDown a TCustomControl, so no amount of publishing gives them TabStop/TabOrder/focus/key events -- that is the root cause behind both the TTyUpDown.ArrowKeys entry and the TTyProgressBar TabStop entry.
- TWO OF THE THREE PAIRINGS COULD NOT BE DIFFED — their LCL counterparts do not exist in any Lazarus installation on this machine, so no evidence-backed comparison was possible and none was invented. (1) TGauge: C:/lazarus/lcl/extctrls.pp contains no TGauge — its full class list is TPage, TNotebook, TUNBPages, TTimer, TCustomIdleTimer, TIdleTimer, TCustomShape/TShape, TCustomSplitter/TSplitter, TPaintBox, TCustomImage/TImage, TBevel, TCustomRadioGroup/TRadioGroup, TCustomCheckGroup/TCheckGroup, TBoundLabel, TCustomLabeledEdit/TLabeledEdit, TCustomPanel/TPanel, TFlowPanel family, TCustomTrayIcon/TTrayIcon, TCtrlBand(s), TCustomControlBar/TControlBar (extctrls.pp:41-1682). `grep -rl "TGauge = class"` and `find -iname "*gauge*"` over all of C:/lazarus, D:/lazarus and D:/fpcupdeluxe/lazarus returned nothing (only an unrelated TMUIGauge widget wrapper in lcl/interfaces/mui/muicomctrls.pas:28). TGauge is a Delphi VCL sample control (Vcl.Samples.Gauges), not shipped with the LCL. (2) TActivityIndicator: `grep -rl "TActivityIndicator"` over the same three installs returned nothing — it is Delphi/FMX-only. So TTyGauge and TTyActivityIndicator have no verifiable their-side surface here; I deliberately did NOT substitute TProgressBar (comctrls.pp) for TTyGauge because the library has a dedicated tyControls.ProgressBar.pas whose group owns that comparison. Both controls do share TTyGraphicControl with the diffed instruments, so gaps 1-10 (AntiAliasingMode, OnPaint, the four drag events, the three horizontal-wheel events, Color) apply to them as well. (3) The "TIndustrialBase family" in this install is only TIndustrialBase (industrialbase.pp:14) plus ONE concrete descendant, TArrow (arrow.pp:33) — `grep -ril TIndustrialBase /c/lazarus` matches just those two files, and there is no components/industrial directory (no TmKnob/TAnalogGauge/TLED etc.; `find -type d -iname "industrial*"` empty in all three installs). The diff for TTyDial/TTyLevelMeter/TTyMeter therefore used TIndustrialBase's own published member plus the surface TArrow demonstrates a concrete family member exposes. (4) TTyAnalogClock was read in full but had no assigned pairing and no LCL counterpart exists (no clock control in the install), so it was not diffed; entries 1-10 nonetheless apply to it via the shared base. EXCLUDED per the task rules: ParentColor (arrow.pp:64) and Color-inheritance plumbing as meaningless for theme-locked self-drawn controls, with the Color name-mismatch reported separately; protected members (TIndustrialBase.GraphicChanged :19, GetControlClassDefaultSize/SetInitialBounds :33) — worth noting only that our constructors set Width/Height directly (e.g. tyControls.Gauge.pas:129-130) instead of overriding GetControlClassDefaultSize, and no ty unit overrides it (grep returned nothing), which I judged not user-facing rather than a gap.
- All seven of our units and all seven listed LCL sources opened and read in full (tyControls.Base.pas read first for the inherited baseline: tyControls.Base.pas:101-141 = TTyGraphicControl published set, :196-246 = TTyCustomControl published set).  NOT CHECKABLE — TTyGridPanel vs TCustomGridPanel/TGridPanel: this Lazarus install ships NO GridPanel at all. `grep -rn "TCustomGridPanel"` over C:/lazarus/lcl/*.pp, *.pas and include/*.inc returned nothing, and `grep -rl "GridPanel"` over C:/lazarus/lcl/ and C:/lazarus/components/ returned nothing (TGridPanel with ColumnCollection/RowCollection/ControlCollection is Delphi-VCL-only; LCL's nearest relative is TCustomFlowPanel/TFlowPanel at C:/lazarus/lcl/extctrls.pp:1309 with a TFlowPanelControlList collection at :1288, which was not in the pairings). I therefore produced NO entries for that pairing rather than invent their-side file:line evidence. Two observations the parent may want to route into a Delphi-sourced pass: (a) TTyGridPanel/TTyGridCell has no ColumnSpan/RowSpan although the pure solver already accepts spans — TyGridCellRect(…, AColSpan, ARowSpan) at tyControls.GridPanel.pas:159-160 — and Relayout hardcodes 1,1 at :606, i.e. capability built and not wired; (b) there is no collection-item surface (no ColumnCollection/RowCollection item class): tracks are a comma-separated string, ColumnSizes/RowSizes at tyControls.GridPanel.pas:120-121.  Also not diffed for want of a pairing/counterpart: TTyRelativePanel (no LCL equivalent), TTyScrollContent (an internal viewport, tyControls.ScrollContent.pas:35), TTyGridCell as a standalone pair.  DUPLICATION POLICY: the 17 TTyPanel/TCustomPanel entries apply verbatim to TTyScrollBox, TTyPaintPanel, TTyGridPanel and TTyRelativePanel, since all four descend from TTyPanel (tyControls.ScrollBox.pas:32, tyControls.PaintPanel.pas:27, tyControls.GridPanel.pas:78, tyControls.RelativePanel.pas:95) and inherit its published set; I filed them once against TTyPanel instead of four times each. OnPaint is filed twice on purpose — once for TTyPanel (assignable-but-dead) and once for TTySplitter, where LCL gives it the extra meaning of suppressing the default look.  DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED per the brief (concept has a themed equivalent, or handle plumbing): Color / ParentColor / ParentBackground / ParentFont / ParentDoubleBuffered / DoubleBuffered / Ctl3D-likes; BorderStyle (C:/lazarus/lcl/controls.pp:2319, published TPanel extctrls.pp:1187 and TScrollBox forms.pp:232 default bsSingle) — the "draw a recessed frame or not" concept is covered by the theme's border tokens plus per-instance StyleOverride (tyControls.Base.pas:244); FullRepaint (C:/lazarus/lcl/extctrls.pp:1165, whose own comment says it "has no effect in LCL"); WSRegisterClass / HandleAllocated / WindowProc / GetClientScrollOffset / ScreenToClient-ClientToScreen offsetting (C:/lazarus/lcl/include/scrollingwincontrol.inc:363-381 — ours bakes the offset into child bounds by design, so the translation is not needed); TCustomPanel/TPaintBox default-size deltas for the panel itself. Confirmed PRESENT on our side and correctly excluded: Align, Anchors, AnchorSide*, Constraints, BorderSpacing, Cursor, Hint, ShowHint, ParentShowHint, PopupMenu, Enabled, Visible, Font, TabOrder, TabStop, Action, Left/Top/Width/Height (published on TControl at C:/lazarus/lcl/controls.pp:1842-1846 and thus inherited), Alignment (default taCenter matches C:/lazarus/lcl/extctrls.pp:1159), Align default alLeft on the splitter (matches :422), AutoSnap default True (matches :423), MinSize default 30 (matches :426), ResizeStyle default rsUpdate (matches :431), Canvas (public via TCustomControl, C:/lazarus/lcl/controls.pp:2484), OnClick/OnDblClick/OnMouse*/OnResize/OnChangeBounds/OnContextPopup/OnKey*/OnEnter/OnExit/OnEditingDone, and hover repainting (theme :hover via CurrentStates, tyControls.Base.pas:1034-1047, matches the splitter's trBandHot at C:/lazarus/lcl/include/customsplitter.inc:872-873).
- All six of our units and all five named LCL sources were opened and read in full; no file failed to open. Extra LCL files read to complete the inherited surfaces: C:/lazarus/lcl/include/shape.inc, C:/lazarus/lcl/include/bevel.inc, C:/lazarus/lcl/include/graphiccontrol.inc, C:/lazarus/lcl/include/control.inc, C:/lazarus/lcl/industrialbase.pp (TArrow's ancestor), C:/lazarus/lcl/comctrls.pp (TGrabStyle), C:/lazarus/lcl/controls.pp. D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/tyControls.Base.pas was read first, so members it publishes for free (Version, Enabled, Visible, Font, Hint, ShowHint, Cursor, ParentShowHint, PopupMenu, Constraints, BorderSpacing, Action, OnClick/OnDblClick/OnMouse*/OnContextPopup/OnResize/OnChangeBounds, StyleClass, StyleOverride, Controller) are NOT reported as gaps.  (1) TLinkLabel / TCustomLinkLabel DO NOT EXIST in this Lazarus install: `grep -rl LinkLabel C:/lazarus/lcl C:/lazarus/components` returns only components/pas2js/pjsdsgnoptsframe.* (that project's own options frame). TTyLinkLabel therefore could not be diffed against a same-named LCL class; I diffed it against TCustomLabel/TLabel as the nearest LCL concept and report only what I could cite there (WordWrap; AutoSize default, folded into the shared entry). VCL-only TLinkLabel members (Elements/TLinkLabelElements, LinkType, LinkColor/HotLinkColor/VisitedLinkColor, UseVisualStyle, OnLinkClick, and <a href> markup inside Caption) are deliberately NOT reported: there is no file:line evidence for them in this install and inventing it would break the evidence rule. A VCL-based link-label diff needs a VCL source tree.  (2) Excluded per the stated rules: ParentColor / ParentFont / ParentBidiMode / DoubleBuffered / ParentDoubleBuffered (theme-locked self-drawn controls); WSRegisterClass / RegisterPropertyToSkip and protected-only members (TCustomLabel.GetLabelText, UpdateSize, DoDrawText, CalculateSize, HasMultiLine, CanTab; TCustomShape.DrawToCanvas, UpdateMask, StyleChanged; TDividerBevel.CalcSize); TCustomLabel's own disabled-text rendering (customlabel.inc:130-140), which we cover with the themed ':disabled { opacity }' rule (source/tyControls.DefaultTheme.pas:294).  (3) Real but low-value, folded here rather than consuming one of the 32 slots the schema allows: (a) TStaticText.TabStop/TabOrder (stdctrls.pp:1148-1149) - TStaticText is a windowed focusable control while TTyLabel is a TGraphicControl by design and has no focus path at all; our TTyCustomControl base does publish TabOrder/TabStop (source/tyControls.Base.pas:210-211), so a focusable static text means re-basing the class. (b) TArrow.AntiAliasingMode (industrialbase.pp:23, default amDontCare) - TTyPainter/BGRA always antialiases, nothing to switch. (c) GetControlClassDefaultSize differences: TShape 65x65 (shape.inc:299-303) vs ours 120x80 (source/tyControls.Shape.pas:125-126); TArrow 20x20 (arrow.pp:223-227) vs ours 120x64 (source/tyControls.Arrow.pas:173-174); TCustomLabel 65x17 (customlabel.inc:102-106) vs no override on ours; TDividerBevel 240x17 (dividerbevel.pas:172-176) vs ours 150 x TyDensityHeight(...,24) (source/tyControls.Divider.pas:138-139); TBevel 50x50 (bevel.inc:61-65) matches ours exactly (source/tyControls.Bevel.pas:131-132). Designer-drop cosmetics only. (d) TArrow's ctor Constraints.MinWidth/MinHeight floor of 8 (arrow.pp:299-300), which we do not set.  (4) The 32-entry cap forced three groupings that would otherwise be separate entries: the drag/dock set (7 properties + 2 dock events in one entry), TShape's Pen and Brush (two sub-objects in one entry), and the five orphan TShapeType values (one entry); TTyArrow's ShadowType/ShadowColor are one entry because they are one feature. Nothing was dropped for lack of confidence - every "absent" claim was grep-verified case-insensitively across the whole D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/ tree first, and two candidates were downgraded when grep proved the capability lives elsewhere: rotated text (source/tyControls.Menu.pas:876 TextOutAngle - built, but wired to no label and not to the divider) and the star shape (source/tyControls.StarShape.pas:36 TTyStarShape - a separate component, so reported as name-mismatch, not absent).
- Read in full: D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/tyControls.Base.pas (all 1209 lines), the complete interface sections of tyControls.Grid.pas (lines 1-2350; the 10500-line implementation was consulted only where semantics had to be verified - CSV titles at 11431-11510, hint gating at 8524-8549, hoAutoResize at 3312-3318, RowDividerAtY at 4004-4018, IsActiveCell at 3810), tyControls.Columns.pas (1-232), tyControls.ValueListEditor.pas (1-222), tyControls.GridCell.pas (all 20 lines). On their side: C:/lazarus/lcl/grids.pas interface in full (lines 40-1974, including TCustomGrid's protected property block 1220-1326 where the republished surface is declared) plus ClearRows/Clear bodies at 10285-10316, and C:/lazarus/lcl/valedit.pas interface in full (1-339).  Three things I could NOT compare, all called out rather than guessed: 1. tyControls.GridCell.pas is not a grid-control unit at all - it is a 20-line compatibility re-export of TTyGridCell from tyControls.GridPanel (a layout container, the TGridPanel/TGridCell family), so it has no counterpart anywhere in grids.pas and produced no entries. If the intent was to pair it against something, that pairing is wrong. 2. TTyValueListEditor descends from TTyListBox (ValueListEditor.pas:85) while TValueListEditor descends from TCustomStringGrid (valedit.pas:120). Everything TValueListEditor inherits from the string-grid line - Cells, Cols, Rows, Objects, ColWidths, Selection, OnDrawCell, OnPrepareCanvas, Options, FixedCols, SaveToCSVStream, the whole grid surface - is therefore absent from ours by construction. I did not emit ~60 duplicate entries for that; the grid-side entries above cover those members once each, and the note here records that they are missing from our value-list editor too. 3. Deliberately excluded per the brief: DB-aware and print members (none in these units anyway); widgetset/handle plumbing (CreateWnd, DestroyHandle, WndProc, WSRegisterClass, EraseBackground, the WM_IME_* handlers at grids.pas:1384-1386); DPI plumbing (FixDesignFontsPPI, ScaleFontsPPI, DoAutoAdjustLayout); and the colour/font properties that have a working themed equivalent in our CSS token system - GridLineColor, FixedColor, FixedGridLineColor, FixedHotColor, SelectedColor, FocusColor, DisabledFontColor, BorderColor, BorderStyle, Flat, TitleStyle, Color/ParentColor, DoubleBuffered/ParentDoubleBuffered, UseXORFeatures. I verified each of those has a token or a resolved-style path (e.g. GridLineColor as a function at Grid.pas:1023 reading the TyGridLine key) before dropping it; the one colour concept with NO themed equivalent, AlternateColor, is reported. Also excluded as too internal to be user-facing: GCache, GridFlags, DragDx, EditorKey, EditorOptions, EditorShowing, AdjustInnerCellRect, CheckPosition, EditorByStyle, the EditorKeyDown/KeyPress/KeyUp/UTF8KeyPress public forwarders, and TVirtualGrid/TGridScroller/TStringCellEditor/TButtonCellEditor/TPickListCellEditor/TCompositeCellEditor (LCL's internal editor classes - our equivalents are the TTy* controls wired at Grid.pas:1453-1459).  Every "absent" claim above was checked with a case-insensitive grep over the whole D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/ directory (not just the four group units), plus obvious synonyms, and the grep outcome is recorded in each ours_evidence field. Two capabilities turned out to exist but be unreachable, and are reported as such rather than as absent: TTyColumns.DistributeSpring/coAutoSpring (zero call sites anywhere) and TTyHeader.Options' hoHotTrack + TTyHeader.Images (published but never read by tyControls.Grid.pas).
- All four source files opened and read in full at the declaration level: D:/Projects/ty-controls/source/tyControls.Base.pas (whole file), tyControls.TreeView.pas (interface 1-661 + the paint/geometry bodies I cite: 1490-1510, 1918, 3660-3830, 4060-4080), tyControls.ListView.pas (interface 1-548 + cited bodies), tyControls.HeaderControl.pas (interface 1-155), plus the two shared models tyControls.Columns.pas (1-232) and tyControls.ListView.Layout.pas (type/API section) and tyControls.ImageCollection.pas (TTyVirtualImageList decl). On their side C:/lazarus/lcl/comctrls.pp was read for TListItem/TListItems/TListColumn/TListColumns/TIconOptions/TCustomListView/TListView (976-1799), TTreeNode/TTreeNodes/TCustomTreeView/TTreeView (2867-3910) and THeaderSection/THeaderSections/TCustomHeaderControl/THeaderControl (3944-4163). NOTE: C:/lazarus/lcl/include/customtreeview.inc does NOT exist (the implementation is C:/lazarus/lcl/include/treeview.inc); I therefore took every LCL declaration from comctrls.pp, which is where the published/public surface is declared, and did not need the .inc bodies except for behaviour I could infer from the declarations. customlistview.inc and treeview.inc were available but not needed for member-level evidence, so no implementation-level semantics beyond the declarations are claimed. Verification method: every "absent" claim was preceded by a case-insensitive grep of the WHOLE source/ directory for the member name plus synonyms; the grep result (including "returned nothing") is quoted in ours_evidence. Three greps caught capabilities that exist but are unwired, and those are reported as semantic-mismatch rather than absent: TTyColumn.ImageIndex + TTyHeader.Images (never painted by either control), the tree's ikSelected/ikState/ikOverlay image kinds (only ikNormal is ever requested), and the Ghosted out-param (set, never read). DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED as out of scope or genuinely N/A, so their absence is not reported: colour properties whose themed equivalent we DO have (BackgroundColor/Color, SelectionColor, SelectionFontColor, HotTrackColor -> the TyTreeNode/TyListViewItem :selected/:hover styles); Ctl3D/ParentColor/ParentFont/ParentBackground/DoubleBuffered/BorderStyle (themed); Canvas/Repaint/HandleAllocated/WSRegisterClass/CreateWnd/ChangeScale and all CN_*/LM_* plumbing; TCustomTreeView accessibility (AccessibilityOn, GetSelectedChildAccessibleObject, GetChildAccessibleObjectAtPos) and TCustomListView AllocBy/FlatScrollBars/FullDrag/ShowWorkAreas; ConsistencyCheck/WriteDebugReport (debug-only); OnCreateNodeClass/OnCustomCreateItem/OnCreateSectionClass (meaningless without the corresponding item classes, which are already reported as absent-subobject); KeepCollapsedNodes and tvoEmptySpaceUnselect (low-value option-set members); TTreeNode.Handle/GetHandle; ImagesWidth/LargeImagesWidth/SmallImagesWidth/StateImagesWidth as separate entries (HiDPI resolution picking — folded into the image-list entries); TListItems.AddItem/InsertItem/IndexOf and TCollection members our collections inherit (verified against C:/lazarus/fpc .../classesh.inc:548-567, which gives TTyListItems Clear/Delete/Insert/Exchange/Move/Count/BeginUpdate/EndUpdate/GetEnumerator, so those are NOT gaps). Two shared gaps are reported once with both classes named in our_class rather than duplicated per control (HideSelection, ToolTips, ScrollBars, GetHitTestInfoAt, the drag-and-drop surface, horizontal wheel); the drag-and-drop entry applies to TTyHeaderControl as well (THeaderControl publishes DragCursor/DragKind/DragMode/OnDragDrop/OnDragOver/OnEndDock/OnEndDrag at comctrls.pp:4126-4147) even though the entry names only the tree and list.
